## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-09-04T16:07:30Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Decision**: Batch 1 of 2 — Q1 the December-window block while R-25's log is absent; Q2 where the attestation lives; Q3 what durably flushed means; Q4 making empty nondeterministic_ops interpretable
**Options**: Q1: A fail closed,B skip-not-pass,C unconditional attestation,D defer | Q2: A TuningRecord field bound to criterion_hash,B separate evidence artifact,C D-number,D defer | Q3: A fsync-rename plus registry both checked,B registry row only,C receipt file only,D defer | Q4: A positive probe,B probe-ran boolean,C fail closed until pin frozen,D defer
**Unit**: models-and-baselines

**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: Q1: C. Unconditional attestation | Q2: A. TuningRecord field bound to criterion_hash | Q3: A. fsync+rename then registry, both checked | Q4: A. Positive probe
**Unit**: models-and-baselines
